### [119] [Moore--Read construction and explicit monodromies of Laughlin states on Riemann surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19619)
*Kiyoon Eum*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了紧黎曼曲面上 ν=1/k 的 Moore-Read Laughlin 态构造，通过 CS/WZW 对应从 U(1)_k Chern-Simons 理论导出其共形块，计算了准空穴输运和 Aharonov-Bohm 通量插入下的显式单值性，并猜想其与绝热和乐一致，从而恢复经典结果（如通量平均霍尔电导 1/k），同时将其与高亏格 Laughlin 态的新近代数几何方法联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laughlin 态在高亏格黎曼曲面上的严格构造及其拓扑性质（单值性、和乐、霍尔电导）此前缺乏统一且系统的描述，本文旨在通过 CS/WZW 对应的框架填补这一空白。

Method: 利用 U(1)_k Chern-Simons/Wess-Zumino-Witten 对应，在紧黎曼面上构造 Moore-Read Laughlin 态共形块；计算准空穴输运及 AB 通量插入下的显式单值矩阵；提出其与绝热和乐一致的猜想；通过定义在 Jacobian 簇上的 Laughlin 态向量丛恢复霍尔电导 1/k；并与代数几何方法进行对比。

Result: 获得了高亏格情形下 Laughlin 态共形块的显式表达式及单值性结果；在识别单值性与绝热和乐的猜想下，恢复了通量平均后的霍尔电导 1/k 这一经典结论；建立了与代数几何构造方法的对应关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为高亏格 Laughlin 态提供了基于 CS/WZW 的系统框架，统一了共形场论与几何/拓扑方法，并为后续验证单值性-和乐猜想及推广到其他分数量子霍尔态提供了基础。

Abstract: We revisit the Moore--Read construction of the $ν=1/k$ Laughlin states on compact Riemann surfaces, deriving their conformal blocks from $U(1)_k$ Chern--Simons theory through the CS/WZW correspondence. We compute their explicit monodromies under quasi-hole transport and Aharonov--Bohm flux insertion, and conjecture that these coincide with the corresponding adiabatic holonomies. Under this identification, we recover classical results on Laughlin states including the flux-averaged Hall conductance $1/k$ via a vector bundle of Laughlin states over $Jac(Σ)$. We also relate our construction to the recently developed algebro-geometric approach to higher genus Laughlin states.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Antipodal constraints on transient Hawking radiation in a conformal channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19244)
*M. Baran Ökten,Aslı S. Turan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究静态时空之外霍金辐射的射线追踪层面行为。在固定参考视界切片上用平衡均匀化定义诱导的对映对合，证明通道标定场中中心反奇结构的产生与湮灭会对共形通道缺陷的空间范数积分施加下界。该下界尺度仅依赖于最大中间失配与诱导对映的雅可比畸变，其余修正项进入需独立控制的余项。圆球标量奇谐波提供解析校准，紧致偶极脉冲饱和几何与时间估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平稳霍金辐射的精细结构，特别是共形通道中与对映奇结构相关的信息编码与破坏机制，需要建立几何约束与通道缺陷之间的关系。

Method: 采用平衡均匀化方法，在固定视界切片上定义非退化平衡分支及诱导对映对合；分析通道标定剥离场中中心反奇结构的产生与湮灭，推导时间积分空间范数下界；利用球面奇标量谐波进行解析校准。

Result: 证明了中心反奇结构产生/湮灭与共形通道缺陷积分范数之间存在下界约束；下界仅依赖最大中间失配和诱导对映的雅可比畸变；灰体滤波、角频率混合、质量效应、自旋依赖和残余传播修正进入需独立控制的余项；圆球偶极脉冲饱和估计。

Conclusion: 建立了非平稳霍金辐射中共形通道缺陷的几何下界，并提供时间平均形式识别出与反剥离振幅除以事件持续时间成正比的瞬态共形通道尺度。

Abstract: Hawking radiation away from stationarity is governed, at the ray tracing level, by the retarded time evolution of the null mapping. We consider an extracted massless conformal channel whose outgoing directions are labelled on a fixed reference horizon slice. Balanced uniformisation, after fixing a nondegenerate balanced branch, defines an induced antipodal involution on this slice. We prove that creation and erasure of centred antipodal odd structure in the channel labelled peeling field enforce a lower bound on the time integrated spatial norm of the conformal channel defect. The forcing scale depends only on the largest intermediate mismatch and the intrinsic Jacobian distortion of the induced antipode, while greybody filtering, angular and frequency mixing, mass effects, spin dependence, and residual propagation or extraction corrections enter through a remainder whose integrated norm must be controlled independently. Round sphere odd scalar harmonics provide analytic calibrations, with the compact dipole pulse saturating the geometric and temporal estimates. A time-averaged form identifies a transient conformal channel scale proportional to the antipodal peeling amplitude divided by the episode duration.

</details>


### [121] [On the physical origin of the radial acceleration relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19245)
*Gary Nash*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出修正广义相对论（MGR）作为解释径向加速度关系（RAR）的统一理论，将暗物质与暗能量统一理解为时空几何的涌现效应，而非独立粒子，从而克服了ΛCDM模型和MOND各自的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 径向加速度关系（RAR）在暗物质主导的星系中仍保持紧密相关性，暗示存在普适的加速度标度。这挑战了ΛCDM标准宇宙学模型，同时MOND虽能部分解释RAR但仍有偏差。因此需要一种全新的理论框架来统一解释引力与暗物质/暗能量的起源。

Method: 引入修正广义相对论（MGR），一种与度量兼容的引力理论，通过连接无关的对称张量Φ_{αβ}描述局域引力能量-动量，完善爱因斯坦方程的总能量-动量张量。将Φ_{00}分量解释为暗能量，Φ_{ij}应力分量解释为暗物质，从暗能量与牛顿力以及暗部门力之间的平衡推导RAR的物理起源。

Result: MGR能够包含MOND作为其子情形，解释宽双星系统中的引力异常，并展示引力在大尺度上呈现观测到的牛顿行为，从而全面解释了RAR的起源与偏差。

Conclusion: MGR为RAR提供了一个几何化的统一解释框架，将暗物质和暗能量视为时空洛伦兹度量本身的基本属性，而非独立实体，可能为超越ΛCDM和MOND的理论发展提供新方向。

Abstract: The radial acceleration relation (RAR) is a tight correlation between the distribution of baryonic matter in galaxies and their observed dynamics, even in systems where dark matter dominates. This suggests a universal acceleration scale exists in galaxies, which appears to challenge the $Λ$CDM standard cosmological model and favor Modified Newtonian dynamics (MOND). However, MOND also deviates from the RAR, so these deviations are not fully explained by the $Λ$CDM model or MOND. A theory that explains gravity and the dark sector geometrically, and treats dark matter as a spin-1 particle, is required, such as Modified General Relativity (MGR). It is the only metric-compatible theory of gravity that describes local gravitational energy-momentum by the connection-independent symmetric tensor $\varPhi_{αβ}$, which completes the total energy-momentum tensor of the Einstein equation. Dark energy is explained by the energy-related component $\varPhi_{00}$, and dark matter by the stress components $\varPhi_{ij}$. Dark matter is extremely difficult to observe because it is fundamentally related to the Lorentzian metric of spacetime itself. The physical origin of the RAR follows directly from the equilibrium between the dark energy and Newtonian forces in the outer part of the rotation curve of galaxies or galaxy clusters, and that between the dark sector forces in the inner part of the rotation curve. It is shown that MGR subsumes MOND, accounts for the gravitational anomaly observed in wide binaries, and exhibits the observed Newtonian behavior of gravity at large scales.

</details>


### [122] [Reconstruction of f(Q,T) Gravity from Logarithmically Corrected Ricci-Gauss-Bonnet Holographic Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19252)
*Preeti Joshi,Ertan Gudekli,Antonio Pasqua,Surajit Chattopadhyay*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在$f(Q,T)=f(Q)+λT$修改引力框架下，研究了宇宙学和热力学行为。通过32个宇宙计时器数据约束幂律背景，构建广义Ricci-Gauss-Bonnet全息暗能量模型，并基于Nojiri-Odintsov熵在视界处分析热力学性质，发现紧凑背景描述与重构有效流体描述给出的熵演化一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非度量性引力（$f(Q,T)$理论）中暗能量的宇宙学与热力学性质，建立全息暗能量与修改引力之间的对应关系，并验证重构模型在视界处的热力学一致性。

Method: (1) 在$f(Q,T)=f(Q)+λT$框架中推导修改Friedmann方程，引入有效暗能量扇区；(2) 采用现象学多项式形式的$f(Q)$，计算状态方程参量和声速平方；(3) 用32个Cosmic Chronometer数据约束幂律背景；(4) 基于Nojiri-Odintsov方案构建广义Ricci-Gauss-Bonnet全息暗能量模型，与有效暗能量密度建立对应；(5) 根据Gibbs关系在表观视界处研究热力学行为，采用Phys. Rev. D 105, 044042 (2022)中的Nojiri-Odintsov熵。

Result: 重构模型的热力学熵演化与紧凑背景描述得到的熵演化高度一致，仅存在微小数值残差，表明该框架在描述全息暗能量、$f(Q,T)$引力与宇宙热力学之间的联系上是自洽的。

Conclusion: 该研究为联系广义全息暗能量、重构$f(Q,T)$引力与宇宙热力学提供了一个自洽的统一框架，验证了模型的经典稳定性及热力学一致性。

Abstract: The current paper reports an investigation on the cosmological and thermodynamic behaviour of an $f(Q,T)$ modified-gravity framework in which the gravitational Lagrangian is written as $f(Q,T)=f(Q)+λT$, with $Q$ denoting the non-metricity scalar and $T$ the trace of the energy-momentum tensor. The modified Friedmann equations are formulated in terms of an effective Dark Energy sector, and the corresponding equation of state and squared speed of sound are examined for a phenomenological polynomial form of $f(Q)$. A power-law background is constrained using 32 Cosmic Chronometer measurements. The resulting background is then used to study the effective Dark Energy dynamics and its classical stability in the redshift range considered. In this study, we further construct a generalized Ricci-Gauss-Bonnet holographic Dark Energy model with logarithmic entropy corrections based on the Nojiri-Odintsov prescription and establish a correspondence between its energy density and the effective Dark Energy density of the $f(Q,T)$ framework. The full function $f(Q,T)$ is then given by adding the trace contribution $λT$. Finally, the thermodynamic behaviour of the reconstructed model is studied at the apparent horizon with the Nojiri-Odintsov entropy motivated by \textit{Phys. Rev. D} \textbf{105}, 044042 (2022) and the Gibbs relation. The entropy evolution obtained from the compact background description is also found to be consistent with that obtained from the reconstructed effective-fluid formulation, with only small numerical residuals. These results provide a consistent framework for examining the connection between generalized holographic dark energy, reconstructed $f(Q,T)$ gravity, and cosmic thermodynamics.

</details>


### [123] [Bayesian constraints on quadratic $f(Q)$ gravity from the ADM mass of white dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19257)
*Edson Otoniel,Lucas A. Sousa,Jonathan A. Rebouças,Juan M. Z. Prete*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(Q)=Q+αQ²的二次对称远程平行引力框架下计算白矮星静态平衡序列，并通过贝叶斯方法对比负耦合、广义相对论和正耦合三个模型。研究发现正耦合使致密分支偏向更大半径和更低质量，负耦合则产生高质量延伸（包括太阳质量达14.34的允许构型）。基于五颗白矮星观测数据，后验概率显示GR(0.378)居于负耦合(0.228)与正耦合(0.395)之间，ln(B_+/GR)=0.043，表明当前数据仅提供耦合参数的约束，尚不足以支持修改引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索修改引力理论（特别是f(Q)引力）是否能够被白矮星观测数据所验证或排除。白矮星作为致密天体，其质量-半径关系对引力理论敏感，是检验广义相对论之外引力理论的理想实验室。同时，作者强调需要严格定义ADM质量表述和可行的恒星域，以避免使用白矮星观测约束修改引力理论时产生伪信号。

Method: 在f(Q)=Q+αQ²引力框架下，通过施加仿射场方程并选取解析分支C=r·exp(B/2)获得规则内部解，并匹配到渐近平坦外部时空以确定ADM质量。物质部分采用冷碳-12状态方程（包含相对论电子简并、核静能和库仑晶格修正）。对固定α的质心密度使用log-uniform先验边缘化。序列覆盖10^14≤|α|/cm²≤10^21，利用五颗白矮星（Sirius B, QS Vir, V471 Tau B, ZTF J1901+1458, LHS 4033）的质量-半径观测数据进行贝叶斯模型比较。

Result: 负耦合与正耦合的后验中位数分别为α_-=-1.12×10^16 cm²和α_+=1.85×10^17 cm²。在等先验权重下，负耦合、GR和正耦合的后验概率分别为0.228、0.378和0.395，ln(B_+/GR)=0.043。正耦合使致密分支偏向较大半径和较低质量，负耦合产生高质量延伸，包括M_ADM=14.34太阳质量的可容许平衡构型。

Conclusion: 当前白矮星观测数据只能对f(Q)引力的耦合参数提供条件性约束，但所有三个模型（负耦合、GR、正耦合）的后验概率处于同一量级且GR并非最劣势模型，说明现有数据不足以提供修改引力的直接证据。若要有效使用白矮星观测约束修改引力理论，必须一致地定义ADM质量表述和可容许的恒星解域。

Abstract: We calculate static equilibrium sequences for white dwarfs (WDs) in quadratic symmetric teleparallel gravity, f(Q)=Q+alpha Q^2, and perform a Bayesian comparison between the negative-coupling sector, general relativity (GR), and the positive-coupling sector using mass-radius measurements. Regular interior solutions are obtained by imposing the affine field equation and selecting the analytic branch C=r exp(B/2), and are matched to asymptotically flat exterior spacetimes to determine the ADM mass. The matter sector is described by a cold carbon-12 equation of state including relativistic electron degeneracy, nuclear rest energy, and Coulomb lattice corrections. Central density is marginalized at fixed alpha with a log-uniform prior. The sequences span 10^14 <= |alpha|/cm^2 <= 10^21. Positive couplings shift the compact branch toward larger radii and lower masses, whereas negative couplings produce high-mass extensions, including an admissible equilibrium configuration with M_ADM=14.34 solar masses. Using Sirius B, QS Vir, V471 Tau B, ZTF J1901+1458, and LHS 4033, we obtain conditional posterior medians alpha_-= -1.12x10^16 cm^2 and alpha_+=1.85x10^17 cm^2. With equal prior weights, the posterior probabilities for the negative, GR, and positive sectors are 0.228, 0.378, and 0.395, respectively, with ln(B_+/GR)=0.043. Current data therefore provide conditional constraints on the coupling but no direct evidence for modified gravity, emphasizing that the ADM mass prescription and admissible stellar domain must be consistently defined when using WD observations to constrain the theory.

</details>


### [124] [On the Tidal and Frame-Drag Fields in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19260)
*Rahulkumar Solanki*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在(1+3)协变分解框架下，针对携带惯性陀螺仪的类时观测者族（构成一个congruence），在有物质存在的情况下定义了潮汐场和框架拖拽场作为约束方程。研究发现，围绕闭合轮廓的相对速度不闭合（由框架拖拽场和流体动量密度导致），陀螺仪自旋的相对方向也不抵消（由潮汐场、流体变量和相对速度的叉积导致）。此外，即使没有框架拖拽场，当相对速度与加速度不平行时，差分Thomas进动也会导致邻近观测者之间的陀螺仪相对进动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真空中，自由下落的邻近类时观测者会因Weyl张量的电部分（潮汐场）和磁部分（框架拖拽场）分别经历相对加速度和陀螺仪相对进动。框架拖拽场没有牛顿对应物。本文的动机是在存在物质时，通过(1+3)协变时空分解，将这两个场作为约束方程加以定义和系统研究，以揭示其与牛顿引力不同的几何与物理效应。

Method: 采用(1+3)协变分裂方法，对一个由携带惯性陀螺仪的类时观测者组成的congruence定义潮汐场（electric part of Weyl tensor）和框架拖拽场（magnetic part of Weyl tensor），将其作为含物质时的约束方程，并分析观测者相对速度和陀螺仪自旋沿闭合轮廓的积分行为。

Result: (1) 围绕由空间分离向量构成的闭合轮廓，观测者的相对速度变化不算为零，偏差由框架拖拽场和流体动量密度贡献。(2) 陀螺仪自旋的相对方向绕同一轮廓不相互抵消，偏差来自潮汐场、流体的各向异性压力和能量密度，以及分离向量相对速度的空间叉积。(3) 即使框架拖拽场为零，当相对速度与加速度不平行时，差分Thomas进动仍会导致邻近观测者间的陀螺仪相对进动。

Conclusion: 论文系统建立了含物质情形下潮汐场和框架拖拽场的约束方程，揭示了相对速度闭合性与陀螺仪自旋取向的不闭合性，并识别出了一种纯运动学效应（差分Thomas进动）可独立于框架拖拽场产生陀螺仪相对进动，深化了对引力磁效应及其类比界限的理解。

Abstract: It is known that in vacuum, two infinitesimally separated timelike observers under free fall notice a relative acceleration due to the tidal field (or the `electric' part of the Weyl tensor), and if they are carrying inertial guidance gyroscopes, they notice relative precession between these gyroscopes due to the frame-drag field (or the `magnetic' part of the Weyl tensor). The frame-drag field thus has no Newtonian analog. In this paper, for a family of timelike observers carrying inertial gyroscopes and forming a congruence, we define these fields as constraint equations in the presence of matter using (1+3) covariant splitting of spacetime. Unlike Newtonian gravity, the relative velocities of the observers around a closed contour formed by their spatial separations (and a temporal vector that closes this contour if the congruence is not hypersurface orthogonal) do not add up to zero due to the frame-drag field and momentum density of the fluid. Moreover, the relative orientations of the gyroscope spins around the same contour do not generally cancel out due to the tidal field, the fluid variables (anisotropic pressure and energy density), and the spatial cross-product of relative velocities associated with the separation vectors that form the contour. Additionally, it is shown that even in the absence of the frame-drag field, neighboring observers see relative precession between their gyroscopes when their relative velocity and acceleration are non-parallel due to differential Thomas precession.

</details>


### [125] [Relational Wigner-Smith Duration in Wheeler-DeWitt Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19261)
*Daulet Berkimbayev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将一个闭合的Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker（FLRW）Wheeler-DeWitt模型表述为精确的反射问题。通过反射相位导数定义关系式跨越持续时间，其前两个时钟矩由协变标量时钟可观测量给出。文章推导了该持续时间的解析表达式、经典再坍缩极限以及领先量子修正。有限谱包还给出了区分两种再坍缩方向的操作性等先验最小错误概率。该构造被扩展到有界有限量子探测器，其多通道反射矩阵产生探针跃迁、谱-探针相关性及矩阵持续时间。直接弱耦合计算验证了预测的跃迁与持续时间标度。结果表明标量时钟条件化和定向几何截面是同一正频率Dirac扇区的局部表示，完全通过相关性和驻定约束态的散射记录定义持续时间和跃迁可观测量，无需引入背景时钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子引力与量子宇宙学中，时间问题（problem of time）是核心难题之一。传统Wheeler-DeWitt方程描述的是驻定约束态，其中缺乏外部背景时间，因此如何定义持续时间、跃迁等可观测量成为关键挑战。本文旨在从封闭FLRW量子宇宙学模型中，通过散射/反射问题的视角，纯粹依赖约束态内部的相关性和散射记录来构造时间与跃迁可观测量，从而避免引入背景时钟。

Method: 本文将闭合FLRW Wheeler-DeWitt模型重新表述为精确的反射问题。利用反射相位导数定义关系式跨越持续时间，并通过协变标量时钟观测量的前两个时钟矩来提取时间信息。文章导出了持续时间的解析表达式及其经典再坍缩极限和量子修正。同时，通过有限谱包构造操作性的等先验最小错误概率，用于区分再坍缩的两种取向。进一步将该构造推广到有界有限量子探测器，利用其多通道反射矩阵推导探针跃迁、谱-探针相关性和矩阵持续时间，并通过直接弱耦合计算验证相关标度关系。

Result: 文章获得了持续时间的解析表达式、经典再坍缩极限及领先量子修正；构造了等先验最小错误概率的判别方案；建立了多通道散射框架下的矩阵持续时间；并通过弱耦合计算验证了跃迁与持续时间的标度律。最后证明了标量时钟条件化与定向几何截面均为同一正频率Dirac扇区的局部表示。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在无需背景时钟的前提下，持续时间和跃迁可观测量可以完全通过驻定约束态的相关性与散射记录来定义和计算。这一结果对量子宇宙学中的时间问题提供了新的视角，表明关系式量子动力学与散射形式主义可以统一地刻画量子宇宙的再坍缩过程及其探测器响应。

Abstract: A closed Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker Wheeler-DeWitt model is formulated as an exact reflection problem. The derivative of its reflection phase defines a relational crossing duration whose first two clock moments follow from a covariant scalar-clock observable. An analytic expression is obtained for this duration, its classical recollapse limit, and the leading quantum correction. A finite spectral packet also gives an operational, equal-prior minimum error probability for distinguishing the two orientations of recollapse with a geometric reading. The construction is extended to a bounded finite quantum detector. Its multichannel reflection matrix yields probe transitions, spectral-probe correlations, and a matrix duration. A direct weak-coupling calculation verifies the predicted transition and duration scalings. Scalar-clock conditioning and oriented geometric sections are shown to be local representations of the same positive-frequency Dirac sector. These results define duration and transition observables entirely through correlations and scattering records of a stationary constrained state, without introducing a background clock.

</details>


### [126] [Renormalization group improved black holes in non-commutative momentum-dependent spacetime geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19264)
*Gema Ilham Baskara Darman,Ar Rohim,Anto Sulaksono*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了动量依赖时空几何中的黑洞，评估其量子Reissner-Nordström（RN）解与弱引力猜想（WGC）的一致性。研究发现在Schwarzschild情形下存在热（非零温度）黑洞残骸，并证明大质量极端RN黑洞具有非零Hawking温度，与WGC一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力效应对黑洞热力学的影响，特别是检验量子修正后的极端黑洞行为是否满足弱引力猜想（WGC），即极端态不应完全稳定或完全冷。

Method: 通过非对易动量空间代数引入量子修正，采用时空重整化方法建立动量空间与时空之间的映射。分别对Schwarzschild和RN解进行热力学分析，并在大质量极限（M远大于普朗克质量）下检验极端情形的Hawking温度行为。

Result: Schwarzschild黑洞在蒸发停止时（熵趋于零）存在热的（非零温度）黑洞残骸，满足热力学第三定律的互补形式。对于大质量RN黑洞，量子修正后的极端几何呈现非零Hawking温度，与WGC关于极端态不应完全稳定或冷的预言一致。动量依赖度规在红外极限下恢复经典Poincaré代数结果。

Conclusion: 量子修正的动量依赖时空几何提供了一种与弱引力猜想自洽的黑洞描述，其红外极限与经典模型一致，从而为量子引力的微观理论提供了热力学层面的支持。

Abstract: We investigate black holes (BHs) in momentum-dependent spacetime geometry and assess its quantum Reissner-Nordström (RN) consistency with the weak gravity conjecture (WGC). Quantum corrections are introduced through the non-commutative momentum space algebra as the quantization process, and spacetime renormalization approach as a map between momentum and spacetime spaces. For the Schwarzschild case, thermodynamic analysis indicates the existence of a hot (non-zero temperature) BH remnant when evaporation stops (the entropy becomes zero), obeying a complementary third law for black hole thermodynamics. We extend this framework to the RN solution and examine its extremal limit. For large BHs with $M \gg M_P$ ($M_P$ for Planck mass), the quantum-improved RN geometry exhibits a non-zero Hawking temperature in the extremal case, consistent with the WGC, which stipulates that extremal states should not be exactly stable or cold. The resulting momentum-dependent metric and thermodynamic properties are shown to reproduce the results derived from the Poincaré algebra (classical model) in the infrared (IR) regime.

</details>


### [127] [Thermodynamics, topology and non-Abelian instability of the five-dimensional Einstein--Yang--Mills black hole with exponential entropy correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19265)
*Aram Bahroz Brzo,Peshwaz Abdulkareem Abdoul,Behnam Pourhassan,Izzet Skalli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯（EYM）黑洞，其度规函数因Wu-Yang ansatz携带对数非阿贝尔贡献。作者推导了质量-半径关系、霍金温度、熵、杨-米尔斯势及第一定律等热力学量，并发现该对数项使中心奇异性更加尖锐。加入非微扰指数熵修正后，解析获得了修正后的热容和自由能。几何在r_h=|Q|处极端，热容在r_h=√3|Q|处发散（Davies点），而修正将该点向内推移。广义离壳自由能揭示了守恒拓扑荷W=0的稳定-不稳定缺陷对。球对称磁扰动分析显示存在纯虚不稳定模，其增长率从0.414753单调降至0.317520，且无限几何塔状不稳定模以电荷无关的比率e^{-π/√2}累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维EYM黑洞中，Wu-Yang ansatz带来的对数非阿贝尔项使度规函数解析可解，为研究规范荷如何重塑四维以上地平线热力学提供了一个理想的解析框架。作者旨在系统刻画该几何的热力学属性、非微扰修正效应、拓扑结构与动力学稳定性。

Method: 从五维EYM-Wu-Yang黑洞出发，解析推导热力学量（质量-半径关系、霍金温度、熵、YM势、第一定律）和曲率不变量。引入非微扰指数熵修正，解析求解修正后热容和自由能。利用广义离壳自由能计算拓扑荷W。对球对称磁YM扰动扇区采用两套独立数值方法求解，验证模谱和累积比。

Result: 对数项使中心奇异性更尖锐而非平滑；几何在r_h=|Q|处极端；热容在Davies点r_h=√3|Q|发散，修正后移至r_tr/|Q|≈√3-9λαη·e^{-3√3λ}。拓扑荷W=0由稳定-不稳定缺陷对承载，仅内部成员响应修正。扰动分析中两种数值方法一致到1.2×10^{-7}，主不稳定模增长率从Γ₀r_h=0.414753（Q→0）单调降至0.317520（Q/r_h=0.99），跨越Davies标度时无特征。有效势衰减系数-9/(4r_*²)超过临界值1/4，支撑无限几何塔状不稳定模以Γ_{n+1}/Γ_n→e^{-π/√2}≈0.108453累积。

Conclusion: 五维EYM-Wu-Yang黑洞在热力学上呈现丰富结构：对数非阿贝尔项同时影响奇异性结构和热力学量；非微扰修正系统性地移动Davies点；拓扑分类为W=0的缺陷对；磁YM扰动扇区存在无限不稳定模谱，其连续谱结构因有效势衰减强度而由幂律而非指数决定。

Abstract: The five-dimensional Einstein--Yang--Mills black hole obtained from the higher-dimensional Wu--Yang ansatz carries a logarithmic non-Abelian contribution to the metric function, making it an analytically tractable setting for studying how gauge charge reshapes horizon thermodynamics beyond four dimensions. We derive expressions for the mass--radius relation, the Hawking temperature, the entropy, the Yang--Mills potential and the first law, and record the curvature invariants, which show that the logarithm sharpens the central singularity rather than smoothing it. A non-perturbative exponential entropy correction is then added, and the corrected heat capacity and free energies are obtained analytically. The geometry is extremal at $r_h=|Q|$ and the heat capacity diverges at $r_h=\sqrt{3}\,|Q|$, a Davies-type point. The correction moves that point inward, $r_{\rm tr}/|Q|\simeq\sqrt{3}-9λαη\,e^{-3\sqrt{3}λ}$. Using the generalized off-shell free energy we find a conserved topological charge $W=0$ carried by a stable--unstable defect pair whose inner member alone responds to the correction. We then solve the spherically symmetric magnetic Yang--Mills perturbation sector, which probes the gauge field itself rather than a test scalar. Two independent numerical schemes agree to better than $1.2\times10^{-7}$ and give a purely imaginary unstable mode whose growth rate falls monotonically from $Γ_{0}r_h=0.414753$ at $Q/r_h\to0$ to $0.317520$ at $Q/r_h=0.99$, passing through the Davies scale without any feature. Because the effective potential decays as $-9/(4r_{*}^{2})$, with a coefficient above the critical value $1/4$, the sector supports an infinite geometric tower of unstable modes accumulating at $Γ\to0$ with the charge-independent ratio $Γ_{n+1}/Γ_{n}\to e^{-π/\sqrt{2}}\simeq0.108453$, which we confirm numerically.

</details>


### [128] [Quasinormal Modes, Partial Transmission Probabilities, and Hod's Conjecture of Charged Black Holes in Perfect Fluid Dark Matter within Kalb-Ramond Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19279)
*Zongyuan Qin,Zheng-Wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kalb-Ramond场诱导的洛伦兹破缺引力框架下，浸没在完美流体暗物质中的带电黑洞的扰动动力学和准正则模(QNM)谱。通过六阶WKB法和时域Prony法交叉验证QNM频率，并利用WKB散射框架计算部分透射概率，发现洛伦兹破缺参数τ对QNM谱影响最强，暗物质参数次之，电荷影响最弱；三种扰动的QNM频率顺序为标量>电磁>引力，而透射概率呈相反顺序；Hod猜想在整个参数范围内均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kalb-Ramond场诱导的洛伦兹对称性破缺对黑洞QNM谱的影响，以及暗物质环境下黑洞扰动特性的变化，旨在深化对洛伦兹破缺引力理论中黑洞物理的理解，并验证相关猜想在非渐近平坦时空中的适用性。

Method: 在KR场诱导洛伦兹破缺引力背景下，构建浸没于完美流体暗物质中的带电黑洞模型（含参数τ、Q/M、λ/M）。在试探场近似下，将标量、电磁、轴向引力三类扰动方程化为含单峰有效势的薛定谔型径向方程，分别采用六阶WKB法和时域Prony法计算QNM频率并交叉验证，同时利用WKB散射框架计算部分透射概率。

Result: 参数扫描显示τ对QNM谱影响最强，λ/M次之，Q/M最弱；相同顺序适用于透射概率。相同参数下，三种扰动的QNM频率满足标量>电磁>引力，透射概率则呈相反顺序，反映有效势垒高度对振荡频率和透射概率的不同影响。Hod猜想|Im(ω)|≤πT_H在整个参数范围内成立。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在KR场诱导的洛伦兹破缺非渐近平坦时空中，黑洞的QNM谱对洛伦兹破缺参数最为敏感，暗物质参数次之，电荷影响最小。不同扰动类型的QNM频率与透射概率呈现相反排序，反映了有效势垒对不同物理过程的不同调控作用。Hod猜想在该非渐近平坦背景下依然成立，拓展了该猜想在更广义引力理论框架下的适用性。

Abstract: Within the Kalb-Ramond field-induced Lorentz-violating gravity, we investigate the perturbation dynamics and quasinormal mode (QNM) spectra of a charged black hole immersed in perfect fluid dark matter. The background spacetime is characterized by the Lorentz-violating parameter tau, the electric charge Q/M, and the dark matter parameter lambda/M. Owing to the non-zero vacuum expectation value of the KR field, the metric function approaches 1/(1-tau) at infinity instead of 1, rendering the spacetime non-asymptotically flat. Under the test-field approximation, the equations of motion for three types of perturbations (scalar, electromagnetic, and axial gravitational) are reduced to Schrodinger-type radial equations with single-peak effective potentials. The QNM frequencies are cross-validated using the sixth-order WKB method and the time-domain Prony method, while the partial transmission probabilities are computed within the WKB scattering framework. Parameter scans show that tau exerts the strongest influence on the QNM spectrum, followed by lambda/M, with Q/M having the weakest effect; the same ordering holds for the partial transmission probabilities. Under the same parameters, the QNM frequencies of the three perturbations follow the order scalar > electromagnetic > gravitational, whereas the transmission probabilities exhibit the reverse order, reflecting the different impact of the effective potential height on the oscillation frequency and on the transmission probability. The Hod conjecture |Im(omega)| <= pi T_H holds throughout the parameter range examined.

</details>


### [129] [Breakdown of Hydrodynamic Universality in Neutron Star Oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19334)
*Gabriel S. Denicol,Amanda Guerrieri,João V. M. Muniz,Gabriel S. Rocha,Raissa F. P. Mendes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出中子星振荡可能无法达到流体动力学普适性（hydrodynamic universality）所需的渐近长波长区域。由于恒星有限半径限制了可访问的波长范围，且因果性对微观弛豫时间施加了下限，微观与宏观尺度之间的分离可能不足以使流体动力学普适性成立。通过比较相对论Navier-Stokes和Israel-Stewart理论中的线性振荡，发现即使在最长的波长下，真实的体粘滞系数也能引起振荡谱的显著修改。这使中子星振荡成为探测超越领头阶流体动力学描述的微观非平衡动力学的直接探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体动力学普适性是相对论耗散流体力学的基础性质，但该性质仅在渐近长波长区域成立。作者希望检验中子星（有限尺度系统）是否真的处于这一普适区域，以及这能否为研究超越标准流体动力学理论的微观非平衡效应提供新的探测途径。

Method: 作者比较了相对论Navier-Stokes理论和Israel-Stewart理论中的线性振荡模型。关键在于分析有限恒星半径（限制波长范围）与因果性约束（限制微观弛豫时间下限）之间的竞争，评估尺度分离是否足以保证流体动力学普适性的成立，并定量计算体粘滞对振荡谱（特别是频率和阻尼）的影响。

Result: 研究发现中子星振荡不需要达到流体动力学普适性区域。由于恒星尺度有限，可访问的波长范围受限，导致微观-宏观尺度分离不充分。在相对论Navier-Stokes与Israel-Stewart两种形式下，真实的体粘滞系数即使在最长波长下也会导致振荡谱的显著差异，表明流体动力学普适性在这些系统中失效。

Conclusion: 中子星振荡可以作为一种直接探针，用于探测超出领头阶流体动力学描述（leading-order hydrodynamic description）的微观非平衡动力学。这一发现对理解致密物质在极端条件下的动力学行为以及天文观测（如引力波信号）的解读具有重要意涵。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic universality refers to the property that different formulations of relativistic dissipative hydrodynamics yield identical predictions in the asymptotic long-wavelength regime. We show that neutron-star oscillations need not reach this regime. Because the finite stellar radius limits the accessible wavelengths and causality imposes a lower bound on microscopic relaxation times, the separation between microscopic and macroscopic scales can become insufficient for hydrodynamic universality to emerge. Comparing linear oscillations in relativistic Navier--Stokes and Israel--Stewart theories, we find that realistic bulk viscosities can produce sizeable modifications of the oscillation spectrum, even at the longest wavelengths. These results identify neutron-star oscillations as a direct probe of microscopic nonequilibrium dynamics beyond the leading hydrodynamic description.

</details>


### [130] [Gravitational-wave propagation constraints on the dynamical Barbero--Immirzi field from GWTC-3 dark sirens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19392)
*Zhi-Fu Gao,Hui Wang,Luiz Carlos Garcia de Andrade*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Barbero-Immirzi（BI）参数从常数提升为动力学标量场后的引力波（GW）传播效应，将其表述为有效摩擦项Γ_BI=ξ_BI H，并与GWTC-3暗标准汽笛数据对Belgacem-Maggiore参数Ξ_0的约束进行比较，得到在约90%置信度下保守有效约束|ξ_BI|≲2.1（即0≤ξ_BI≲2.1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BI参数原本是爱因斯坦-卡特理论中Holst项的常数，将其推广为动力学标量场后需要检验其可观测效应。作者旨在利用现有LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA引力波数据，对该动力学BI扇区给出观测约束，且不依赖天体物理源激发传播性扭波模式的假设。

Method: 将动力学BI扇区对引力波传播的领先阶效应表述为有效摩擦项Γ_BI=ξ_BI H，并关联到引力波光度距离；利用代表红移映射和高斯近似处理LVK公布的GWTC-3暗标准汽笛对Ξ_0的后验约束（Ξ_0=1.2^{+0.7}_{-0.7}），无需重复LVK分层贝叶斯分析即可导出对ξ_BI的约束。

Result: 得到约90%置信度下保守有效约束|ξ_BI|≲2.1；由于该BI实现中ξ_BI在领先阶非负，单侧解释为0≤ξ_BI≲2.1。该可观测约束是对BI诱导引力波摩擦的陈述，将其转化为微观涨落振幅需要独立的BI-扭力扇区归一化，文中未做此经验归一化。

Conclusion: 该引力波传播测试探测的是一个与扭力波和物质扇区约束互补的新扇区，并且不依赖天体物理源激发传播性扭力模式的假设，为动力学BI理论提供了新的观测检验途径。

Abstract: The Barbero--Immirzi (BI) parameter, originally a constant in the Holst term of first-order gravity, can be promoted to a dynamical scalar field $γ(x)$ coupled to torsion in Einstein--Cartan theory. We formulate the leading gravitational-wave (GW) propagation effect of this dynamical BI sector as an effective friction term, $Γ_{\rm BI}=ξ_{\rm BI}H$, and relate it to the GW luminosity distance. We then compare the resulting distance-redshift relation with the published LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA GWTC-3 dark-siren constraint on the Belgacem--Maggiore parameter $Ξ_{0}=1.2^{+0.7}_{-0.7}$. Without repeating the LVK hierarchical Bayesian analysis, and using a representative-redshift mapping together with a Gaussian approximation to the quoted posterior, we obtain the conservative effective constraint $|ξ_{\rm BI}|\lesssim 2.1$ at approximately 90\% credibility. For the specific BI realization considered here, $ξ_{\rm BI}$ is non-negative at the leading order adopted, so the corresponding one-sided interpretation is $0\leqξ_{\rm BI}\lesssim 2.1$. The observable constraint is therefore a statement about the effective BI-induced GW friction. Translating it into the microscopic fluctuation amplitude requires an independent normalization of the BI--torsion sector; we do not impose such a normalization phenomenologically. This GW-propagation test probes a sector complementary to torsion-wave and matter-sector constraints and does not rely on an assumption that astrophysical sources excite propagating torsion modes.

</details>


### [131] [Spacetimes via Continuous posets: Old foundations and new developments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19502)
*Ettore Minguzzi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文向读者介绍连续偏序集（continuous posets）方法在时空几何中的应用，系统回顾了Martin和Panangaden开创的理论，并给出了新结果：确定连续偏序集与因果条件的唯一对应关系（如$\le=D_p$对应"过去区分和未来反射"），证明双连续偏序集恰好等价于因果连续时空，全局双曲偏序集精确刻画全局双曲时空。此外引入推广的Kronheimer-Penrose拓扑因果空间概念，证明多个完备化构造（Lawson完备化、未来GKP完备化、Gigli等人的定向完备化）之间的等价性，并提出时空在基本层面上应为余连续偏序集的物理主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将领域论（domain theory）中的连续偏序集方法与Lorentzian几何和广义相对论中的因果结构理论联系起来，为低正则性（low-regularity）条件下的时空几何提供严格的数学基础。作者希望超越Martin和Panangaden已有的充分性结果，建立必要性结果，从而在因果结构、偏序关系和连续性质之间建立精确等价的刻画。

Method: 作者首先从标准领域论来源整理了所有相关证明，以降低来自Lorentzian几何和广义相对论读者的理解门槛。在时空解释下，将way-below关系与时间序关系$I$等同，推导出$\le$和因果关系条件的唯一选择。引入了广义Kronheimer-Penrose拓扑因果空间概念，涵盖文献中的Lorentzian长度空间，并给出使其成为（双）连续偏序集的弱条件。比较了多种完备化构造（Lawson round-ideal完备化、未来GKP完备化、Gigli等人的定向完备化），证明了它们的等价性，并移除了前人所需的额外假设条件（SC条件和前向逼近性质）。

Result: （1）在way-below关系与$I$等同的框架下，$\le=D_p$是唯一选择，对应"过去区分和未来反射"条件；双连续偏序集等价于因果连续时空（$\le=D$）；全局双曲偏序集精确对应全局双曲时空（$\le=J$），补全了必要的必要性定理。（2）新的拓扑Kronheimer-Penrose因果空间框架足够广泛以涵盖Lorentzian长度空间。（3）Lawson round-ideal完备化等于未来GKP完备化；Gigli等人的定向完备化也在移除额外条件后等价于未来GKP完备化。（4）基于过去反射在黑体蒸发中的关键作用，提出时空基本层面应为余连续偏序集。

Conclusion: 连续偏序集理论为时空几何提供了统一且低正则性的数学框架，其优雅之处在于完全以偏序集的语言表述。论文不仅完善了已有的充分必要性对应关系，还统一了多种边界完备化构造，并提出余连续偏序集作为基础时空模型的物理动机，为后续将领域论工具应用于量子引力和黑洞物理开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce the reader to the continuous posets approach to spacetime geometry, a topic pioneered by Martin and Panangaden. We provide all relevant proofs from standard domain-theory sources to ease the transition for readers from Lorentzian geometry and general relativity. We then present new results for the spacetime interpretation. Under the identification of the way-below relation with the chronological relation $I$, we determine the unique choice for $\le$ and causality condition to obtain specific poset properties. Continuous posets require $\le=D_p$ and correspond to "past-distinction and future-reflectivity"; bicontinuous posets are causally continuous spacetimes with $\le=D$; globally hyperbolic posets are precisely globally hyperbolic spacetimes with $\le=J$ proving a necessity result beyond Martin and Panangaden's sufficiency. The theory, being phrased entirely in terms of a poset, is of low-regularity. We introduce a notion of topological Kronheimer-Penrose causal space that is sufficiently general to encompass the Lorentzian length spaces in the literature, and give weak conditions making it a (bi)continuous poset. Once a spacetime is a continuous poset, domain-theoretic constructions apply directly, e.g., completion schemes yield spacetime boundaries. We recall a few; the most natural for preserving continuity, Lawson's round-ideal completion, is proved to equal the future GKP completion. The directed completion recently studied by Gigli et al. was also proved to be equivalent to the future GKP completion; however with additional SC condition and forward approximation which we are able to remove. Finally, physical considerations on the recently established key role of past-reflectivity in black hole evaporation lead us to suggest that the spacetime is, at the fundamental level, a co-continuous poset.

</details>


### [132] [Optimal Extension Regularity at the McVittie Event Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19581)
*Yi-kun Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了正宇宙学常数与常状态方程参数barotropic流体驱动的时空平坦McVittie解中，未来黑洞事件视界的局部扩展正则性问题。通过分析测地线仿射长度与曲率张量的渐近行为，确定了视界扩展的最优正则性等级，揭示了从C^2不可扩展到解析扩展的算术层级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于精确确定McVittie时空（描述嵌入FLRW宇宙中的黑洞）未来视界处可允许的局部扩展正则性。此类时空在视界处的几何奇异行为直接关系到广义相对论中"扩展为何种正则性"这一根本问题，且结果在物理上可解释为宇宙学衰变速率与视界红移之比对几何正则性的决定性作用。

Method: 论文方法包括：(1) 对径向零测地线进行仿射参数化分析，确定其有限仿射长度到达视界；(2) 计算平行传播（parallelly propagated）角向曲率分量的渐近行为，得到C s^{p-2}的形式，以此排除低正则性扩展；(3) 对p≥2的情形构造参数一致的Gaussian-null紧化及显式双面Lorentzian collar；(4) 利用Hölder类C^{N,θ}的标准理论与解析延拓技术，分别处理非整数和整数情况，并分析临界值p=2处边界Einstein自同态的幂零结构。

Result: 主要结果：对于0<p<2，不存在锚定C^2扩展；对于非整数p=N+θ（N≥2），最优正则性恰为C^{N,θ}；每个整数p≥2落入"解析岛"，允许实解析局部扩展；临界值p=2时边界Einstein自同态含非零秩一幂零部分。这一算术层级结构完全由p=3(1+w)H_∞/κ决定。

Conclusion: 宇宙学衰减与视界红移之比的相对大小决定了黑洞视界扩展正则性的严格算术层级：从无C^2扩展到Hölder类、再到实解析的可扩展性，形成以参数p为指标的离散"分辨谱"，揭示了McVittie时空在视界处的几何奇异行为本质上是正则性（而非光滑性）层面的现象。

Abstract: We determine the optimal local extension regularity of the future black-hole event horizon in the exact spatially flat McVittie solutions sourced by a positive cosmological constant and a barotropic fluid with constant equation-of-state parameter $w>-1$. Let $H_\infty$ be the asymptotic Hubble constant, $κ$ the surface gravity of the limiting black-hole root, and $p=3(1+w)H_\infty/κ$. Ingoing radial null geodesics reach the horizon in finite affine length. A parallelly propagated angular curvature component is asymptotic to $C s^{p-2}$, with $C\ne0$ and $s$ the remaining affine distance, which excludes every anchored $C^2$ extension for $0<p<2$. For $p\ge2$ we construct a parameter-uniform Gaussian-null compactification and an explicit two-sided Lorentzian collar. If $p=N+\vartheta$ is nonintegral, with $N\ge2$ and $0<\vartheta<1$, the optimal regularity is the standard big Hölder class $C^{N,\vartheta}$: extensions of this class exist, whereas no $C^{N,\vartheta'}$ extension exists for $\vartheta'>\vartheta$. Every integer $p\ge2$ instead belongs to an analytic island and admits a real-analytic local extension. At the critical value $p=2$ the boundary Einstein endomorphism has a nonzero rank-one nilpotent part. The ratio of cosmological decay to horizon redshift therefore determines a sharp, arithmetic hierarchy of geometric regularity.

</details>


### [133] [A New Method for Quasinormal Modes From Bound States and Homotopy deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19597)
*Hao-Yun Ma,Bo-Yun Zhou,Jia-Hui Huang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 受Mashhoon束缚态方法启发，提出了一种通过两步坐标变换引入实参数α，将准正规模（QNM）问题映射为束缚态问题的新方法，通过解析延拓反演得到QNM频率。该方法首次从倒置Regge-Wheeler势的束缚态谱直接计算Schwarzschild黑洞的QNM频率，对低阶模式（overtone n≤ℓ）精度高，但对高泛音精度下降。通过Padé近似分析复α平面上的奇异点结构，发现高泛音的奇异点位于解析延拓圆内，解释了方法局限性的根源。提出同伦变形改进方法，可可靠计算更多高阶模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的QNM计算方法在处理高泛音（overtone）时面临数值稳定性和精度问题。受Mashhoon束缚态方法的启发，作者希望建立一种将QNM问题转化为束缚态问题的新方法，从而利用束缚态谱计算的成熟技术来高效、精确地求解QNM频率，并深入理解该方法的适用范围和局限性。

Method: 该方法的核心包括：(1) 通过两步坐标变换引入实参数α，将QNM问题映射为束缚态本征值问题；(2) 束缚态本征值E_n(α)通过解析延拓反演为QNM频率ω_n；(3) 利用Padé近似在复α平面上分析E_n(α)的奇异点结构，以诊断方法的适用性；(4) 提出对势函数进行同伦变形的策略，以移动奇异点位置，改善高泛音的计算精度。数值验证针对Schwarzschild黑洞的倒置Regge-Wheeler势展开。

Result: 该方法对低阶模式（n≤ℓ）能给出高精度的QNM频率，这是首次直接从倒置Regge-Wheeler势的束缚态谱计算得到Schwarzschild QNM。但对高泛音，精度下降或计算失败。Padé分析显示高泛音对应的E_n(α)奇异点落在解析延拓圆内，直接从数学结构上解释了这一局限。通过同伦变形势函数，成功将方法扩展至更多高阶模式。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一种将QNM问题映射为束缚态问题的新计算方法，并通过解析延拓与Padé分析揭示了其适用范围的深层原因。同伦变形作为改进策略，能有效扩展方法的适用范围。这项工作的意义在于加深了对束缚态方法适用于QNM计算的理论理解，并为处理高泛音QNM提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Inspired by Mashhoon's bound state method, we propose a new bound state method for computing quasinormal modes (QNMs). By a two-step coordinate transformation where a real parameter $α$ is introduced, a QNM problem is mapped to a bound state problem, whose eigenvalues $E_n(α)$ are inversely mapped to the QNM frequencies $ω_n$ via analytic continuation. With this method, we numerically calculate various QNM frequencies for a Schwarzschild black hole directly from the bound state spectrum of the inverted Regge-Wheeler potential for the first time. It is found that the method yields QNM frequencies of high accuracy for low-lying modes with overtone $n\leq\ell$ ($\ell$ is the multipole number), while the accuracy degrades or the calculation fails for higher overtones. To identify the origin of this limitation, we analyze the singularity structure of the eigenvalues $E_n(α)$ using Padé approximants in the complex $α$-plane. For higher overtones, the singularities of $E_n(α)$ lie within the analytic continuation circle, providing a direct explanation for the limitation of the method. To mitigate this limitation, we suggest a homotopy deformation to the potential, which improves the method and enable us to compute a few more high overtone modes reliably.

</details>


### [134] [Generalized Polytropic Regular Black Holes in Arbitrary Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19606)
*Seyed Naseh Sajadi,Supakchai Ponglertsakul,Petarpa Boonserm,Orlando Luongo,Hernando Quevedo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在任意时空维度下研究了具有反德西特（AdS）渐近行为的静态球对称规则黑洞，其由各向异性流体驱动，径向压力对应真空能而切向压力满足广义多方状态方程。作者推导了一类通用的渐近AdS黑洞解并确定了时空正则性条件，在薄壳框架内研究了其动态形成，同时在任意维度下验证了热力学第一定律和Smarr关系，并在四、五、六维时空下分析了热力学稳定性与相结构，展示了维度依赖的相变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则黑洞是解决经典广义相对论中奇点问题的重要途径，而渐近AdS黑洞在AdS/CFT对偶框架下具有重要物理意义。作者旨在任意维度下构建一般性的规则AdS黑洞解，并系统研究其动态形成机制与热力学性质，以揭示维度对黑洞物理的影响。

Method: 该方法包含三个层次：(1) 通过求解爱因斯坦场方程，利用径向压力为真空能、切向压力满足广义多方状态方程的各向异性流体源，推导出一般的渐近AdS黑洞解并确定正则性条件；(2) 在薄壳形式主义下，假设壳层物质满足线性正压状态方程，研究这类规则黑洞的动态形成过程；(3) 验证热力学第一定律和Smarr关系，并在四、五、六维下系统分析热力学稳定性与相结构。

Result: 成功得到了一类通用的渐近AdS规则黑洞解及其正则性条件；在薄壳框架下证实了该类黑洞可以通过动态过程形成；在任意维度下热力学第一定律和Smarr关系均得到验证，并在四、五、六维中揭示了维度依赖的不同相变行为，表明黑洞的相结构随时空维度变化而改变。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了任意维度下的一般性规则AdS黑洞解族，并从动力学形成与热力学两个角度验证了其物理自洽性。特别地，维度依赖的相变结构表明黑洞热力学行为与时空维度密切相关，为理解规则AdS黑洞及其在更高维引力理论中的应用提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We investigate static, spherically symmetric regular black holes with anti-de Sitter (AdS) asymptotics in arbitrary spacetime dimensions. They are solutions of Einstein gravity, sourced by an anisotropic fluid whose radial pressure corresponds to vacuum energy, while the tangential pressure satisfies a generalized polytropic equation of state. By solving the Einstein field equations, we derive a generic class of asymptotically AdS black hole solutions and determine the conditions required for spacetime regularity. We then investigate the dynamical formation of these regular black holes within the thin-shell formalism, assuming a linear barotropic equation of state for the shell matter. Next, we study the thermodynamics of the regular AdS black holes in arbitrary dimensions by verifying the first law of black hole thermodynamics and the corresponding Smarr relation. We analyze the thermodynamic stability and phase structure of solutions in four, five, and six spacetime dimensions, demonstrating the existence of dimension-dependent phase transitions.

</details>


### [135] [Open quantum system approach to the Unruh-DeWitt detector in impulsive plane wave spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19692)
*Hing-Tong Cho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用开放量子系统框架和影响泛函形式，对与无质量标量场相互作用的Unruh-DeWitt探测（谐振子）在脉冲平面波时空中的响应进行了非微扰分析，并计算了探测器的期望值和跃迁概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在平面波时空中Unruh-DeWitt探测器对引力波和电磁波的非微扰响应，特别是研究这两种不同性质的波如何影响探测器的量子跃迁行为。

Method: 采用开放量子系统框架结合影响泛函形式主义，通过减去Minkowski时空结果，非微扰地计算$\langle Q^2\rangle$、$\langle P^2\rangle$和$\langle \{Q,P\}\rangle$的期望值，以及从基态到第一激发态的跃迁概率$P_{0\rightarrow 1}$。分别对纯引力波（Ricci张量为零）和零电磁波（Weyl张量为零）两种情形进行显式计算。

Result: 在纯引力波和零电磁波两种情形下，波都会抑制探测器的激发跃迁。由于采用非微扰方法，该方法同时适用于弱耦合和强耦合常数的情况。

Conclusion: 脉冲平面波（无论是纯引力波还是零电磁波）对Unruh-DeWitt探测器的激发跃迁具有抑制作用，这一结论在非微扰框架下对弱耦合和强耦合均成立。

Abstract: In this paper we employ the open quantum system framework with the influence functional formalism to non-perturbatively analyze the response of an Unruh-DeWitt detector modeled as a harmonic oscillator which interacts with a massless scalar field in impulsive plane wave spacetimes. Subtracting the Minkowski results, we obtain the expectation values for $\langle Q^2 \rangle$, $\langle P^2 \rangle$, and $\langle \{Q,P\} \rangle$, along with transition probabilities $P_{0\rightarrow 1}$ from ground state to the first excited state of the detector due to the influence of the wave. Explicit calculations are performed for both a pure gravitational wave (vanishing Ricci tensor) and a null electromagnetic wave (vanishing Weyl tensor). In both scenarios, the wave suppresses excitation transitions. Since our approach is non-perturbative, we are able to consider cases with both weak and strong coupling constants.

</details>


### [136] [Can Stochastic Clocks in FLRW Minisuperspace Prevent Dynamical Singularities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19695)
*Pradosh Keshav MV*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了Wheeler-DeWitt方程的随机扩展，在FLRW迷你超空间中展示了量子回反应可以通过动力学机制调节大爆炸奇点，而无需引入外部边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典量子宇宙学中的Wheeler-DeWitt方程面临奇点问题，通常需要人为施加边界条件来处理大爆炸奇点。作者希望建立一个自洽的动力学机制，使奇点回避自然产生，而不是作为外部约束被强加。

Method: 采用Laplace-Beltrami量子化方法，并对粗粒化引力子模式进行开放系统处理，推导出随机哈密顿演化方程。扩散系数取σ(a)∝a²的形式（乘性噪声在原点消失），然后利用Feller边界分类方法分析a=0处成为入口边界的性质，并考察各宇宙学扇区下大尺度因子的全局行为。

Result: 扩散系数在原点消失使得a=0成为Feller分类中的入口边界，导致Laplace-Beltrami加权平稳密度呈超指数抑制，且奇点处概率通量为零。在大尺度因子下，de Sitter和正势能主导区域呈现幂律平稳尾部；而限制势或负有效宇宙学常数则在大尺度因子处形成入口边界，给出全局可归一化的稳态。

Conclusion: 由半经典粗粒化产生的随机回反应为迷你超空间量子宇宙学中的奇点回避提供了一致且动力学化的机制，无需外部边界条件。

Abstract: We develop a stochastic extension of the Wheeler--DeWitt equation in FLRW minisuperspace and show that quantum backreaction can dynamically regulate the big bang singularity without imposing external boundary conditions. Using Laplace--Beltrami quantisation and an open-system treatment of coarse-grained graviton modes, we obtain a stochastic Hamiltonian evolution equation in which the diffusion coefficient takes the form $σ(a)\propto a^2$. This multiplicative noise vanishes at the origin and renders $a=0$ an entrance boundary in Feller's classification, leading to super-exponential suppression of the Laplace--Beltrami weighted stationary density and zero probability flux into the singular point. At large scale factor, the global behaviour depends on the cosmological sector: de Sitter and positive potential-dominated regimes exhibit power-law stationary tails, whereas confining potentials or negative effective cosmological constant lead to an entrance boundary at infinity and a globally normalizable steady state. Taken together, these results indicate that stochastic backreaction arising from semiclassical coarse-graining provides a consistent and dynamical mechanism for singularity avoidance in minisuperspace quantum cosmology.

</details>


### [137] [Finite-Momentum Kinetic Corrections to Viscous Tensor Perturbations in an Expanding Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19755)
*Nishil Savla,Gurudatt Gaur*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平坦FLRW宇宙中，粘性相对论介质中传播的张量扰动（引力波），重点分析了超越局部因果弛豫模型的空间自由流效应。基于Baym-Patil-Pethick的弛豫时间玻尔兹曼方程，在零流极限下张量应力满足Maxwell-Cattaneo（即线性Müller-Israel-Stewart）关系；保留空间流项后，对极端相对论各向同性介质解析求解角响应，并将动力学响应与张量爱因斯坦方程耦合在物质+Λ背景中数值求解。数值结果显示转移函数出现非单调修正（约+0.8%于x≈0.37和-5.6%于x≈1.97），且有对应的有限动量相位修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部因果弛豫模型（如Müller-Israel-Stewart）虽然广泛用于描述粘性介质对引力波的响应，但忽略了空间自由流效应。本文旨在通过完整的动能方法研究这一空间流项对张量扰动传播的影响，以检验局部模型的适用性并揭示超越局部描述的新物理效应。

Method: 从Baym-Patil-Pethick的弛豫时间玻尔兹曼方程出发，先证明零流极限下退化为Maxwell-Cattaneo/MIS关系；随后保留空间流项，对极端相对论各向同性介质解析地求出角响应；将得到的动能响应耦合到张量爱因斯坦方程中，在平坦物质+Λ背景下进行数值求解，并与局部MIS传播模型和独立的动能WKB计算进行对比验证。

Result: 完整数值计算产生对张量功率转移函数的非单调修正：在x≈0.37处有约+0.8%的极大值，在x≈1.97处有约-5.6%的极小值。这些特征通过角分辨率和ODE公差测试验证稳定，并被独立的动能WKB计算复现。同时发现相应的有限动量相位修正。

Conclusion: 空间自由流项在张量扰动传播中产生非平凡的、非单调的修正效应，且该效应依赖于所采用的单弛豫时间动能模型的特定性质，不应将其推广为普适的输运定律。该研究为引力波在粘性介质中传播的精确描述提供了动能框架的参考基线。

Abstract: We study tensor perturbations propagating through a viscous relativistic medium in a spatially flat FLRW universe, with particular emphasis on the correction produced by spatial free streaming beyond a local causal relaxation model. We start from the relaxation-time Boltzmann equation used by Baym, Patil, and Pethick to describe the response of matter to a gravitational wave. In the zero-streaming limit, the tensor stress obeys a Maxwell-Cattaneo, or linear Muller-Israel-Stewart, relation. We then retain the spatial streaming term and evaluate the resulting angular response analytically for an ultrarelativistic isotropic medium. The resulting kinetic response is coupled to the tensor Einstein equation and solved numerically in a flat matter-plus-Lambda background, with comparison to the local MIS propagation model and an independent kinetic WKB calculation. For the illustrative normalization considered, the full numerical calculation produces a nonmonotonic correction to the tensor power transfer function, with a maximum of about 0.8 percent near x equal to 0.37 and a minimum of about minus 5.6 percent near x equal to 1.97. These features are stable under angular-resolution and ODE-tolerance tests and are reproduced by the kinetic WKB calculation. We also find a corresponding finite-momentum phase correction. The effect is a property of the specified single-relaxation-time kinetic model and should not be interpreted as a universal transport law.

</details>


### [138] [Galactic microlensing by Lobo-Parsaei-Riazi phantom wormhole: Paczyński light curves and probabilistic features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19757)
*G. F. Akhtaryanova,R. Kh. Karimov,R. N. Izmailov,U. K. Khidirov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界Lobo-Parsaei-Riazi幻影虫洞的银河系微引力透镜效应，推导了弱场偏转角至四阶后牛顿项，计算了爱因斯坦半径、穿越时间、光变曲线、光学深度和事件率，并比较了有质量幻影分支与无质量情形（γ=1）及史瓦西黑洞的观测差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力微透镜可能提供区分有质量与无质量幻影虫洞的观测方法，为检验/识别虫洞这类替代致密天体提供可能的观测途径。

Method: 假设源星位于银河系核球和大麦哲伦云，推导LPR幻影虫洞弱场偏转角至四阶后牛顿精度，计算爱因斯坦半径、爱因斯坦半径穿越时间和理想点源Paczyński型光变曲线；在简化模型中假设虫洞透镜引力束缚于银河系，估算光学深度和事件率。

Result: LPR参数γ（与径向状态方程参数ω=1/γ相关）影响ADM质量、爱因斯坦半径和微透镜时标。有质量幻影分支（-1<γ<0）中，主导偏转项正比于1/r，点源光变曲线呈Paczyński型；无质量情形（γ=1）在定性上不同，主导1/r项消失，光变曲线可能出现"槽"（gutter）结构。

Conclusion: 有质量幻影虫洞的微透镜信号与史瓦西黑洞类似，而无质量情形（γ=1）呈现出显著不同的定性特征（光变曲线出现槽结构），可作为区分虫洞类型的潜在观测判据。

Abstract: Gravitational microlensing can provide a possible observational method for distinguishing between the signatures of massive and massless phantom wormholes. In this work, we consider Galactic microlensing by the bounded Lobo-Parsaei-Riazi phantom wormhole (LPR), assuming source stars located in the Galactic Bulge and in the Large Magellanic Cloud (LMC). We derive the weak-field deflection angle up to fourth post-Newtonian order and compute the Einstein radius, Einstein-radius crossing time, and idealized point-source Paczyński-type light curves. We also estimate the optical depth and event rate in a simplified model in which the wormhole lenses are assumed to be gravitationally bound to the Galaxy. The LPR parameter $γ$, which is related to the radial equation-of-state parameter by $ω= 1/ γ$, affects the ADM mass, the Einstein radius, and the microlensing timescale. In the massive phantom branch $-1 < γ< 0$, the leading deflection term is proportional to $1/r$, and the resulting point-source light curves are Paczyński-like. The massless comparison case $γ= 1$ is qualitatively different because the leading $1/r$ term vanishes and gutters may appear. The idealized observational predictions are compared with those for a Schwarzschild black hole.

</details>


### [139] [How Inflation Might Explain Large-Scale Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19765)
*Brett McInnes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文表明，若放宽时空挠率为零的限制，扭转暴涨理论可以自然产生宇宙大尺度各向异性，这并不与暴涨图景矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的观测分析暗示宇宙可能存在大尺度各向异性，这常被认为与早期暴涨图景相矛盾。作者希望表明这种观点是错误的。

Method: 采用1+3分解分析扭转时空，区分内禀挠率与外禀挠率。基于挠性暴涨理论，考虑暴胀子各向异性的量子涨落产生各向异性的外禀挠率场。

Result: 外禀挠率在暴涨期间不断增长，而内禀几何的空间截面被常规方式各向同性化。内禀挠率（随暴涨衰减）与外禀挠率竞争导致暴涨早期存在挠率引起的能量通量，在各超视界模留下各向异性信号，最终在当今宇宙的大尺度上显现。

Conclusion: 大尺度各向异性并不违背暴涨理论，而是可以在扭转暴涨中自然、几乎不可避免地产生。

Abstract: Several recent observational analyses have suggested the possible existence of a large-scale anisotropy in our Universe. It is often thought that this would contradict the inflationary picture of the earliest times. We show that this is not the case: if we relax the artificial restriction that spacetime torsion must be zero, then such anisotropies arise naturally, indeed almost inevitably, in a theory of torsional Inflation. This is seen most clearly if one uses a ``$1\,+\,3\,$'' analysis of a torsional spacetime, leading naturally to concepts of intrinsic torsion and extrinsic torsion. An anisotropic quantum fluctuation of the inflaton leads to an extrinsic torsion field which is anisotropic and which grows throughout the inflationary era, even though the intrinsic geometry of the spatial sections is isotropised in the conventional manner. The competition between the intrinsic torsion (which does inflate away) and the extrinsic torsion leads to a short interval, early in the inflationary era, during which there is a torsion-induced energy flux, which leaves an anisotropic signal in superhorizon modes and thus ultimately at large scales in the present Universe.

</details>


### [140] [Kerr Quasinormal Modes without Variable Separation: A Two-Dimensional Hyperboloidal Teukolsky Solver with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19774)
*Antonio Ferrer-Sánchez,Daniela D. Doneva,José D. Martín-Guerrero,Stoytcho S. Yazadjiev,Roberto Ruiz de Austri-Bazan,Yolanda Vives-Gilabert,José A. Font*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用物理信息神经网络（PINNs）直接在二维双曲坐标Teukolsky方程框架下求解Kerr时空中引力准正则模（QNM）的本征值问题，无需分离变量，在近极端极限下依然保持亚0.5%的精度，为不可分离的超越Kerr黑洞微扰问题提供了灵活的数值求解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Kerr黑洞QNM计算依赖于Teukolsky方程的变量分离，但对于超越Kerr黑洞（如非可分旋转背景、Kerr-Newman引力-电磁耦合微扰），扰动方程不可分离，需要一种不依赖分离变量的数值方法。

Method: 采用物理信息神经网络（PINNs）直接求解二维超boloid坐标下Teukolsky方程的本征值问题，保留径向-角向耦合结构，无需分离变量；通过构造递增角动量的序列逼近极端极限。

Result: 对基础模(2,0,0),(2,1,0),(2,2,0),(3,3,0),(4,4,0)及第一泛音(2,2,1)进行求解，所有实部和虚部频率分量误差低于0.5%，中位偏差0.1%；精度在近极端区域（阻尼率极小、模态寿命最长）依然保持。

Conclusion: 该方案提供了一条非谱方法求解多维黑洞微扰本征问题的数值路径，虽不及专用Kerr求解器的精度，但灵活性更高、分析预处理更少；不可分旋转背景及Kerr-Newman引力-电磁耦合系统是该方法的自然扩展方向。

Abstract: We use physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) to solve the gravitational quasinormal-mode (QNM) eigenvalue problem for Kerr spacetime directly in the two-dimensional hyperboloidal formulation of the Teukolsky equation. This formulation does not require separation of variables and thus retains the coupled radial--angular structure. Such a scheme provides a prototype for calculating the QNMs of beyond-Kerr black holes for which the perturbation equations are non-separable. Sequences with increasing angular momentum are constructed, reaching close to the extremal limit. We focus on the fundamental modes $(\ell,m,n)=(2,0,0)$, $(2,1,0)$, $(2,2,0)$, $(3,3,0)$ and $(4,4,0)$, together with the first overtone $(2,2,1)$. Independent benchmark evaluation shows that every reported real and imaginary frequency component remains below $0.5\%$ error, with a median deviation of $0.1\%$. This accuracy is maintained in the near-extremal regime, where the damping rate becomes small and the modes are longest-lived. The results establish a non-spectral numerical route to multidimensional black-hole perturbation eigenproblems which does not match the substantially higher precision of dedicated Kerr solvers but offers greater flexibility and requires less analytical pre-processing. Non-separable rotating backgrounds and coupled systems, such as gravitational--electromagnetic Kerr--Newman perturbations, are natural extensions of the same construction.

</details>


### [141] [Universal Relations for Neutron Stars from Asymptotic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19939)
*Syo Kamata,Josuke Minamiguchi,Shuhei Minato*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从星体结构微分方程出发，解析推导了中子星无量纲可观测量（转动惯量、潮汐形变率、自旋诱导四极矩、紧致度）满足的I-Love-Q和Love-C普适关系，发现这些普适关系仅依赖于三个参数（两个积分常数和一个最外层多方指数），并解释了为何在现实物态方程范围内偏差保持在百分量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星的无量纲可观测量（如转动惯量、潮汐形变率等）在不同物态方程下满足I-Love-Q和Love-C普适关系，精度可达百分量级，但此前这一普适性的起源尚未被从解析角度充分理解。

Method: 作者从缓慢旋转、受潮汐形变的恒星的结构微分方程和边界条件出发，对一般的分段多方物态方程推导这些普适关系的渐近展开。通过筛选与观测到的普适性一致的展开形式，分析这些关系的解析结构，并据此对普适性的破坏进行分类。

Result: 发现普适关系仅依赖于三个参数：两个积分常数和最外层星壳的多方指数。在现实物态方程占据的参数空间内，这些参数对普适关系的扰动导致的偏差保持在百分量级，与观测精度一致。同时建立了普适性破坏的分类框架。

Conclusion: 该形式化方法仅依赖底层的微分方程和边界条件，因此可以推广到其他物理系统，为理解和预测类似普适关系的存在性提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Dimensionless observables of neutron stars, such as the moment of inertia, the tidal deformability, the spin-induced quadrupole moment, and the compactness, satisfy the I--Love--Q and Love--$C$ universal relations to percent-level accuracy over a wide range of equations of state. We investigate analytically the origin of this insensitivity in the stellar structure equations. We derive asymptotic expansions of these relations directly from the differential equations and boundary conditions for slowly rotating, tidally deformed stars described by a general piecewise-polytropic equation of state. Although the differential equations allow several forms of the asymptotic expansion, we find that only one class is consistent with the observed universality, and we use this class to analyze the universal relations. Because the observables are determined at the stellar surface, information about the deep-interior equation of state can enter them only through the parameters that survive in the asymptotic expansion at the surface. We find that the universal relations depend only on three parameters: two integration constants and one polytrope index of the outermost segment. By determining how these parameters deform the relations, we show that, throughout the region of parameter space occupied by realistic equations of state, the resulting deviations remain at the percent level, consistent with the observed accuracy of the universal relations. Within the same formalism, we classify violations of universality according to the additional degrees of freedom or input data responsible for them. Since the construction relies only on the underlying differential equations and boundary conditions, the same procedure can be applied to other systems once the corresponding equations and boundary conditions are specified.

</details>


### [142] [Dynamics of Charged Radiating Collapse with Shear and Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19963)
*A. Khalid,Muhammad Bilal Riaz,S. A. Mardan,Mustafa Inc*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦框架下研究了一个带电、各向异性、辐射、含剪切和热流的恒星引力坍缩模型，通过将内部时变球对称时空与外部带电Vaidya时空匹配，将带电结条件化为Riccati型微分方程并求得精确解，系统分析了能量密度、压力、热流、电场、能量条件、声速、Herrera开裂判据及复杂度因子等物理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带电各向异性恒星在剪切和热流存在下的引力坍缩过程，以更全面地理解真实恒星的辐射坍缩物理行为，特别是电磁场对各向异性、热输运和复杂度因子的影响。

Method: 采用爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦框架，构建内部时变球对称几何与外部带电Vaidya时空的匹配；通过麦克斯韦方程显式纳入电磁场；将带电结条件经适当变换化为Riccati型微分方程并求得精确解。

Result: 能量密度和径向压力为正并随距表面距离递减，切向压力为负，确认各向异性特征；热输运和电磁效应在恒星内部最强；能量条件和因果性条件均被满足；开裂函数表明模型对开裂具有潜在稳定性；复杂度因子为正，电荷在压力各向异性、密度不均匀性和耗散热流之外提供了额外贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了带电剪切辐射坍缩模型物理行为与内部结构的完整图像，验证了模型的物理合理性和稳定性，并揭示了电磁场对复杂度因子的显著贡献。

Abstract: We investigate a charged anisotropic radiating stellar configuration undergoing gravitational collapse in the presence of shear and heat flux within the Einstein Maxwell framework. The interior spacetime is described by a time dependent spherically symmetric geometry and is matched to an exterior charged Vaidya spacetime. The electromagnetic field is incorporated explicitly through Maxwell equations, allowing the electric charge to contribute to the matter variables, mass function, and boundary evolution. The charged junction condition is reduced to a Riccati type differential equation with suitable transformations and exact solution is obtained. The physical properties of the resulting shearing solution are examined through the energy density, radial and tangential pressures, pressure anisotropy, heat flux, electric charge, energy conditions, sound speeds, Herrera cracking criterion, and complexity factor. The energy density and radial pressure remain positive and decrease towards the stellar surface, whereas the tangential pressure remains negative, confirming the anisotropic character of the configuration. Heat transport and electromagnetic effects are strongest in the inner stellar region. The energy and causality conditions are satisfied. The cracking function indicates potential stability against cracking. The complexity factor remains positive, with electric charge providing an additional contribution alongside pressure anisotropy, density inhomogeneity, and dissipative heat flux. These results provide a comprehensive picture of the physical behavior and internal structure of the charged shearing radiative collapse model.

</details>


### [143] [Analyzing Quasi-normal modes spectrum in asymptotically de Sitter black bounces and their optical appearance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20081)
*G. Alencar,Albert Duran-Cabacés,A. Lima,Ángel Rincón,Diego Sáez-Chillón Gómez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过Simpson-Visser方案构造的渐近de Sitter黑洞弹跳时空，统一描述了规则黑洞、极端构型和可穿越虫洞。作者验证了时空的正则性（曲率不变量有限、测地线完备），分析了有效应力-能量张量（NEC违反，源于幻影标量场与非线性电动力学复合源），并通过数值方法研究了阴影、光子环和轴向引力微扰的类正则模频率，揭示了宇宙学常数与正则化参数a之间的非平凡耦合及其观测判别潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑洞奇点被视为广义相对论的病态特征，而黑洞弹跳模型提供了一种无需奇异性的替代方案。作者希望将Simpson-Visser正则化方案推广到包含宇宙学常数（de Sitter背景）的情形，构建一个统一的几何框架来连接规则黑洞、极端构型与可穿越虫洞，并探索这些几何在观测上（阴影、光子环、引力波谱）是否具有可区分的特征。

Method: 1) 通过Simpson-Visser替换方案构造渐近de Sitter黑洞弹跳解族；2) 计算Kretschmann、Ricci等曲率不变量验证正则性，分析测地线完备性；3) 剖析有效应力-能量张量，将其解释为幻影标量场耦合非线性电动力学的复合源；4) 采用数值光线追踪研究零测地线传播、阴影半径及光子环结构；5) 使用伪谱方法计算轴向引力微扰的类正则模频率及其泛音。

Result: 结果表明：该时空对所有非零正则化参数a均保持曲率正则且测地线完备；NEC在有效源中被违反；宇宙学常数与正则化尺度a之间的相互作用显著影响光环结构和类正则模特性的光谱；这些差异可作为区分规则时空与奇异时空的潜在观测判据。

Conclusion: 渐近de Sitter黑洞弹跳时空构成一个自洽的正则黑洞模型族，其观测特征（阴影结构、光子环与类正则模频率）与奇异Schwarzschild-de Sitter解存在可探测的差异，为未来引力波与高分辨率电磁观测提供了区分规则与奇异引力的潜在判别信号。

Abstract: This work explores asymptotically de Sitter black-bounce spacetimes, obtained as a regular extension of the Schwarzschild-de Sitter geometry via the Simpson-Visser prescription. The resulting family of solutions provides a smooth interpolation between regular black holes, extremal configurations, and traversable wormholes within a single geometrical framework. In particular, we verify the regularity of the spacetime by showing that (some of) curvature invariants, including the Kretschmann and Ricci scalars, remain finite at the origin for any non-vanishing value of the regularization parameter $a$, ensuring geodesic completeness. An analysis of the effective stress-energy tensor reveals a violation of the null energy condition, which can be consistently interpreted in terms of a composite source involving a phantom scalar field coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics. Subsequently, we investigate the observational signatures of these geometries by studying: i) null geodesic propagation and ii) constructing the associated optical appearance. In particular, we determine the shadow radius and characterize the structure of photon rings through numerical ray-tracing procedure. Finally, we compute the spectrum of axial gravitational perturbations using a pseudospectral approach, obtaining the quasinormal mode frequencies and their overtones. Our results show a non-trivial interplay between the cosmological constant and the regularization scale $a$, which imprints itself on both the light-ring structure and the quasinormal spectrum. These features suggest potential observational discriminants that could distinguish these regular spacetimes from their singular counterparts in future gravitational-wave and high-resolution electromagnetic observations.

</details>


### [144] [Effective Field Theory for Freezing Gravity with Minimally Coupled Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20085)
*Zhibang Yao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对冻结引力（FG）这一幻影穿越暗能量模型进行宇宙学扰动分析，将其与理想流体最小耦合，推导出无鬼影和无梯度不稳定性的条件，并证明FG的两大特征（背景与扰动分离、大尺度标量自由度退化为非动力学）在有物质存在时依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FG模型此前仅在无物质（真空）条件下被提出和研究，但现实宇宙中物质是不可避免的组成部分。因此需要研究FG与完美流体耦合时的稳定性和动力学行为，以验证该模型在实际宇宙学场景中的可行性。

Method: 通过详细的宇宙学线性扰动分析，在不取高k极限的情况下解析推导鬼影稳定性条件，得到无鬼影的尺度范围和有限截断。同时在高k极限下分析梯度稳定性条件，并在准静态区域内推导有效引力耦合和引力滑移，用EFT参数表达结果。

Result: （1）解析得到了无鬼影的尺度范围和有限截断，真空分支中物质耦合会降低截断值；（2）高k区间的梯度稳定性条件保证了冷暗物质具有正的有效引力耦合；（3）FG的两个核心特性在引入物质后依然保持：背景与扰动分离、大尺度标量自由度在领头阶退化为非动力学；（4）在准静态区域导出了有效引力耦合与引力滑移。

Conclusion: FG模型在引入物质后依然保持稳定的扰动动力学和其独特结构，但大尺度标量自由度退化的行为是否在非线性阶仍然成立、从而避免强耦合问题，需要通过非线性扰动分析进一步探究。

Abstract: Freezing gravity (FG), a phantom-crossing dark energy model, was recently proposed in the absence of matter. In this work, we study FG minimally coupled to a perfect fluid through a detailed cosmological perturbation analysis and derive the conditions for the absence of ghost and gradient instabilities. The ghost stability conditions are obtained analytically without taking the high-$k$ limit, yielding the ghost-free range of scales and a finite cutoff when it exists. In the branch where a finite cutoff is already present in vacuum, we find that matter coupling lowers it relative to its vacuum value. Moreover, in the high-$k$ regime, the gradient stability condition guarantees a positive effective gravitational coupling for cold dark matter. We further show that two characteristic properties of FG persist in the presence of matter. First, the background and perturbation sectors remain separated, in the sense that they are controlled by independent sets of parameters, allowing FG to realize arbitrary background evolutions, including phantom crossing, while maintaining stable perturbation dynamics. Second, the scalar degree of freedom of FG becomes non-dynamical in the large-scale limit at linear order, while propagating with a finite speed of sound on small scales. Whether this behavior persists to arbitrarily high orders in perturbation theory, thereby avoiding the potential strong-coupling issue, requires further investigation through a nonlinear perturbation analysis. To make contact with large-scale structure and gravitational lensing observables, we also derive the effective gravitational coupling and gravitational slip within the quasi-static regime and express our results in terms of the EFT parameters.

</details>


### [145] [Spin-Inversion Degeneracies in Restricted Inspiral Waveforms for LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20174)
*Kata Karácsonyi,László Árpád Gergely*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在受限波形模型下研究了超大质量双黑洞旋进（inspiral）中自旋翻转（spin inversion）效应能否从引力波数据中区分出来。结果表明，在准圆二阶后牛顿频率演化并叠加LISA灵敏度加权的受限波形下，各种自旋翻转情形与无翻转双黑洞系统高度简并（最大匹配度超过0.999865），即使在信噪比ρ*=100时也无法区分。作者指出需要更完整的波形（包括观测者参照系中的进动调制、高次谐波、独立偏振和完整LISA响应）才能检验这种简并能否被打破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋翻转在多个理论框架中有不同预测（解析工作预测不等质量双黑洞的单一翻转，数值相对论和后牛顿计算显示近似等质量双黑洞存在重复flip-flop运动，而长期自旋演化还预测了自旋诱导质量四极矩导致的新情形）。这些效应能否在引力波数据中被区分仍是未解问题，本文旨在通过构建受限波形来研究这一可区分性。

Method: 将长期自旋角度方程与准圆二阶后牛顿（2PN）频率演化相结合，构建由天空平均LISA灵敏度加权的受限波形。研究了五个近等质量注入信号（探测器框架总质量M=2×10^5 M_sun），包括Kerr翻转情形和一个四极矩诱导情形。每个注入信号与受限于无轨道面穿越的物理演化波形进行比较，搜索所有四个无翻转扇区，变化质量、自旋幅度和初始自旋角度，并在时间、相位和整体幅度上最大化。

Result: 大自旋运动本身并不会导致明显不同的波形。一个弱自旋扫过133.26°的情形在参考信号SNR ρ*=100时残差SNR为0.806；双自旋多次穿越轨道面的情形产生最大残差1.641；四极矩情形的次自旋范围128.11°且有五次穿越，但最佳无翻转候选的残差SNR仅为0.454。所有最大匹配度均超过0.999865。

Conclusion: 在该受限模型内，自旋翻转情形在ρ*=100时与无翻转双黑洞强烈简并，无法区分。需要引入更完整的波形要素（观测者参照系进动调制、高阶谐波、分离偏振和完整LISA响应）来进一步检验这种简并是否可被打破。

Abstract: Spin inversions appear in several settings. Analytical work predicts a single spin flip during unequal-mass supermassive binary inspiral, while numerical relativity and post-Newtonian calculations show repeated flip-flop motion in comparable-mass binaries. Secular spin evolution also predicts additional cases driven by spin-induced mass quadrupoles. Whether these effects can be distinguished in gravitational-wave data is still unclear. We combine the secular spin angle equations with a quasi-circular second post-Newtonian frequency evolution and build a restricted waveform weighted by the sky-averaged LISA sensitivity. We study five near-equal-mass injections with detector-frame total mass $M=2\times10^5M_\odot$, including Kerr flip-flops and one quadrupole-induced case. Each injection is compared with physically evolving waveforms constrained to have no orbital-plane crossings. We search all four no-inversion sectors, vary the masses, spin magnitudes, and initial spin angles, and maximize over time, phase, and overall amplitude. Large spin motion does not by itself lead to a clearly different waveform in this restricted model. A weak spin that sweeps through $133.26^\circ$ leaves a residual SNR of $0.806$ at reference signal SNR $ρ_\star=100$. A case where both spins cross the orbital plane many times gives the largest residual, $1.641$. The quadrupole case has a secondary-spin range of $128.11^\circ$ with five crossings, yet the best no-inversion candidate found leaves a residual SNR of only $0.454$. All largest matches found exceed $0.999865$. Within this restricted model, the spin inversion cases are therefore strongly degenerate with no-inversion binaries at $ρ_\star=100$. More complete waveforms, including observer-frame precession modulations, higher harmonics, separate polarizations, and the full LISA response, are needed to test whether this degeneracy can be broken.

</details>


### [146] [A Buchdahl solution generator for Einstein-sigma gravity and multiscalar-tensor gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20180)
*David S. Pereira,Francisco S. N. Lobo,José Pedro Mimoso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将Buchdahl变换形式化为D>3维爱因斯坦引力与非线性sigma模型耦合的靶协变解生成器，通过循环坐标范数的单参数重分布精确生成Ricci源，并结合目标流形的测地线映射得到精确的爱因斯坦-sigma模型解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Buchdahl变换为从真空时空构造标量场解提供了几何途径，但以往机制缺乏统一的靶协变表述。作者希望将该机制推广到更一般的非线性sigma模型，并阐明几何源与靶空间实现之间的清晰分离。

Method: 在Ricci平坦种子时空的连通区域上，若存在非类空、超曲面正交的循环坐标且其范数非零定号，则该范数的对数定义Buchdahl势Σ。对循环范数做单参数重分布，精确生成Ricci源R_AB=C_D(β)∂_AΣ∂_BΣ，其中C_D(β)=(D-2)/(D-3)(1-β²)。将Σ与非退化靶度量下仿射参数化的测地线复合，当测地线速率的平方等于C_D(β)时即得精确爱因斯坦-sigma模型解。讨论了正定与不定靶空间的区别，并通过爱因斯坦/Jordan框架对应给出真空张量-多标量解。

Result: 该生成器统一覆盖了平坦、球面、双曲和轴子-膨胀子靶空间，以及Tangherlini、Weyl、Kasner、Rosen波、Gowdy、Kaluza-Klein气泡和C-度量等种子时空。正定靶情形下非平凡标量映射具有一维像；不定靶情形下测地线ansatz仍是充分解生成扇区但非穷尽。

Conclusion: 该结果将几何Buchdahl源与靶空间实现清晰分离，将秩一循环扇区推广至静态、宇宙学、波动和加速几何，为生成爱因斯坦-sigma模型解提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Buchdahl transformations provide a geometric route from vacuum spacetimes to scalar-field solutions. We formulate this mechanism as a target-covariant solution generator for Einstein gravity coupled to nonlinear sigma models in $D>3$ dimensions. On any connected region in which a Ricci-flat seed admits a non-null hypersurface-orthogonal cyclic coordinate with fixed-sign nonzero norm, the logarithm of that norm defines a Buchdahl potential $Σ$. A one-parameter redistribution of the cyclic norm then generates the exact Ricci source $R_{AB}=C_D(β)\partial_AΣ\partial_BΣ$, with $C_D(β)=\frac{D-2}{D-3}(1-β^2)$, while $Σ$ remains harmonic in the deformed metric. Composing $Σ$ with an affinely parametrized geodesic of a non-degenerate target metric whose squared speed is $C_D(β)$ therefore yields an exact Einstein--sigma-model solution. For positive-definite targets, the corresponding pullback condition also implies that the nontrivial scalar map has one-dimensional image; for indefinite targets, the geodesic ansatz remains a sufficient solution-generating sector but need not exhaust all possible realizations of the same rank-one spacetime source. Through the Einstein/Jordan-frame correspondence, the construction gives vacuum tensor--multiscalar solutions as well. We illustrate the unified generator with flat, spherical, hyperbolic and axion--dilaton targets and with Tangherlini, Weyl, Kasner, Rosen-wave, Gowdy, Kaluza--Klein bubble and C-metric seeds. The result separates cleanly the geometric Buchdahl source from its target-space realization and extends this rank-one cyclic sector across static, cosmological, wave and accelerating geometries.

</details>


### [147] [An Analytical Two Incompressible Fluid Star with a Mixed Ordinary Dark Matter Core and an Ordinary Matter Envelope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20185)
*Milko Estrada,Santiago Esteban Perez Bergliaffa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个解析可求解的广义相对论双流体恒星模型：由普通物质与暗物质共存的混合核心（两者为独立守恒的不压缩完美流体）以及仅含普通物质的外壳组成，推导出压力与度规函数的显式表达式，并给出了与中心压力发散相关的Buchdahl型临界紧致度，该临界值取决于相对暗物质密度和混合核心的大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质与普通物质共存的天体物理系统（如暗物质混合中子星）在现实建模中通常需要复杂的数值处理，缺乏解析可控的参考模型。作者旨在提供一个可解析求解的双流体恒星系统，作为理解暗物质组分对恒星结构影响的理论基准。

Method: 构造混合核心（普通与暗两种独立守恒的不压缩完美流体）+单流体外壳（仅普通物质）的解析两层模型，流体间仅通过时空几何相互作用而无物质/能量交换。通过求解爱因斯坦场方程获得压力与度规函数的显式表达式，进而分析物理容许的参数空间、中心压力发散对应的临界紧致度，并进行质量-半径分析。

Result: 确定了物理容许的参数空间，推导出依赖相对暗物质密度与混合核心尺寸的Buchdahl型临界紧致度；在单流体极限下精确恢复Schwarzschild常密度星及其标准临界值2M/R=8/9。质量-半径分析表明，相同全局紧致度的构型可对应不同的内部物质分布。

Conclusion: 该解析双流体恒星模型为暗物质混入中子星等更复杂系统提供了有用的理论基准，可揭示暗物质分数与核心相对尺寸对恒星结构影响的定性趋势，为后续更现实的数值建模提供参照。

Abstract: We construct an analytical relativistic two fluid star characterized by a mixed core, where ordinary matter and dark matter coexist as two independently conserved incompressible perfect fluids, and an envelope composed exclusively of ordinary matter. The fluids exchange neither matter nor energy and interact only through the common spacetime geometry, with the ordinary component extending across the core envelope interface while the dark component is confined to the core. Despite the mixed core--single-fluid envelope structure and the internal interface, the system remains analytically tractable, allowing us to obtain explicit expressions for the pressures and metric functions and to follow directly the effects of the dark-matter fraction and relative core size. We determine the physically admissible parameter space and derive a Buchdahl like critical compactness associated with the divergence of the central pressure, whose value depends on the relative dark matter density and the size of the mixed core. The Schwarzschild constant density star and its standard critical value, $2M/R=8/9$, are recovered in the corresponding one fluid limit. The mass--radius analysis further shows that configurations with the same global compactness can correspond to distinct internal matter distributions. Beyond providing an analytically controlled description of a core-confined second component, the construction offers a useful benchmark for identifying qualitative trends that may subsequently be examined in more realistic dark matter admixed neutron star models, whose detailed treatment lies beyond the scope of the present work.

</details>


### [148] [The 2PN Point-Mass N-Body Equations of Motion in Harmonic Gauge: A Computable Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20193)
*Hongkun Huang,Jie Yang,Wei-Tou Ni*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种谐和规范下后牛顿2PN阶N体系统运动方程的半解析半数值公式，将运动方程分解为闭合解析部分和非闭合积分部分，并应用于太阳-木星-土星及太阳-水星-金星系统的数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后牛顿（PN）近似是研究相对论性多体问题的重要工具，2PN阶运动方程在强场与弱场系统中均有应用价值。然而，2PN阶N体运动方程中的某些项具有非闭合的积分形式，难以直接数值求解。本文旨在开发一种将解析与数值方法相结合的公式化框架，以处理此类非闭合项的奇异结构并使其可用于实际天体系统计算。

Method: 论文在Hadamard正则化框架下推导了谐和规范2PN方程，将运动方程拆分为闭合解析贡献与非闭合积分贡献两部分；对非闭合积分贡献的奇异结构进行分析，并通过正则化将其转化为可数值求值的形式。随后将所提公式应用于太阳-木星-土星和太阳-水星-金星两个基准系统，沿牛顿轨道计算非闭合2PN加速度的瞬时值及其对应的有限时间相对距离响应（通过摄动方程求解）。

Result: 在两个基准系统中，非闭合加速度仅占完整2PN加速度的很小比例；其诱导的相对距离摄动保持振荡特性，且在后期的振荡幅度可能达到较大值。

Conclusion: 该半解析半数值公式能有效处理2PN阶N体运动方程中非闭合积分项的奇异性与可计算性问题，且在实际太阳系多体系统中非闭合项的相对贡献虽小但不可完全忽略，其长期振荡效应值得进一步关注。

Abstract: We develop a semi-analytic and semi-numerical formulation of the harmonic-gauge second post-Newtonian (2PN) equations of motion for a general point-mass N-body system within Hadamard regularization. The equations of motion are separated into a closed analytic contribution and a non-closed integral contribution. We analyze the singular structure of the latter and further regularize it into a numerically evaluable representation. We apply the formulation to the Sun-Jupiter-Saturn and Sun-Mercury-Venus systems, evaluating the instantaneous non-closed 2PN acceleration along Newtonian trajectories and its leading finite-time relative-distance response through the corresponding perturbation equations. In both benchmarks, the non-closed acceleration remains a small fraction of the complete 2PN acceleration, while the induced relative-distance perturbation remains oscillatory and can reach larger oscillation amplitudes at later times.

</details>


### [149] [Gravitational-wave parameter estimation with machine-learning generated surrogate waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20222)
*Suyog Garg,Kipp Cannon*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段确定性条件自编码器模型来生成SEOBNRv4引力波波形，并与波形条件化步骤相结合，实现低精度代理波形在参数估计任务中的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三代引力波探测器（如爱因斯坦望远镜）将检测到大量具有偏心轨道和高质量比等复杂特征的信号，这些信号的参数估计计算成本极高。由于似然计算需要生成理论波形预测，加速波形生成可显著提升参数估计效率，因此需要更快的波形生成方法。

Method: 提出两阶段确定性条件自编码器模型：第一阶段生成波形的振幅和相位序列，第二阶段校准预测中的残差误差。此外提出波形条件化步骤以支持下游参数估计任务。

Result: 模型与目标偏振波形的中位失配度约为10^-2，校准后的振幅/相位序列达到10^-6量级的余弦距离误差。参数估计测试显示，使用ML波形恢复EOB目标参数时后验存在系统性偏差，该偏差可被估计并校正，通过重要性重加权可在低信噪比下使用低精度代理波形。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的代理波形可以加速引力波参数估计，但存在系统性偏差，需要通过偏差校正和重要性重加权来实现低信噪比下的可靠使用。

Abstract: The worldwide network of gravitational-wave detectors have detected more than 350 binary coalescence events till date. Future third-generation detectors, like Einstein telescope, are expected to detect orders-of-magnitude more signals from sources with more complicated characteristics, including eccentric orbits and high-mass ratio binaries. It is well-established that the computational cost of parameter estimation for signals from these kinds of sources will be extremely high. In particular, the process could be sped-up if generating theoretical waveform predictions, used for likelihood calculation becomes faster. Recently, various machine-learning techniques has been proposed to this end. In this work, we propose a two-stage deterministic conditional-autoencoder model for generating four-parameter SEOBNRv4 waveforms. The first-stage of the model generates amplitude and phase series of the waveform, while the second-stage calibrates the residual error in the predictions. Our model achieves a median mismatch of around $10^{-2}$ with the target polarization waveforms, while the calibrated amplitude/phase series achieve $10^{-6}$ level cosine distance error. We then propose a waveform conditioning step to enable use of these surrogate waveforms for downstream parameter estimation tasks. Finally, we perform extensive parameter estimation tests, with ML and EOB waveform injections and try to recover posterior estimates for the source parameters. We find that when ML waveforms are used to recover EOB target parameter estimates, the inferred posterior have some systematic bias. This inherent bias can be estimated and corrected for, and then importance reweighting of posterior samples can enable use of low-accuracy surrogate waveforms at low SNRs.

</details>
